- **000426_ask-with-options**: An agent must ask the owner a question with concrete options, with the recommended option first. - **000427_report-bugs**: An agent must report everything that can be a bug, and the owner decides. The agent must check it first. It must not drop a borderline finding as intended. - **000428_no-unasked-features**: If an asked feature already exists, an agent must say where it is and must ask before it builds more. It must not add texts or features that nobody asked for. - **05-debugging**: - `pc` and `lr` are module-relative (`main+…`, `sdk+…`) or host symbols.
